GeoGuard 美术总图册 · r02

2026-10-02 · 四制作组已主审accepted；integration汇总submitted，待主审。

38张本轮规范板 · 48身份 · 375身体与外部效果归属 · 95默认/幸存技能。入口均指向最终已批组规范页。
375原画参考动作不等于375独立动画。挂点proposed；原画通过≠透明动画资源、代码接入、设备或实机测试通过。

生效规格 · 覆盖图中文字

SENTINEL LEFT180旧简写已覆盖：整个局部rig绕固定root镜像，body/脸/脚/部件/P/M一起镜像，再在镜像P作剩余瞄准；RIGHT默认，UP按获批构造处理投影遮挡。
B05及B06/B07文字：浅绿/浅色按钮与标签使用深棕#4B281C，B05白字浅绿示例不生效。五组规定实色对比7.00–12.38；真实合成及设备排版另验。
完整生效规格与字号

覆盖核查 · 完整契约JSON · 生产映射 · 通信队列 · 阶段总结

最终规范入口

friendly r03 · enemies r02 · bosses-mechanics r03 · effects-ui r02

已批原画板

28身体板＋1独立实体关系补充＋9效果/UI；旧探索图不混作最终body。

375逐动作来源

概念关键格仅静态参考；MOVE序列、方向构造、外置等级和姿态共享均显式标注，未导出连续资源。

真实玩家UI

组件规范格功能边界
GameHudR01/HUDHP,money,wave/time,pause,boss members/action/phase/counterplay; retain accepted sample
BuildBarR01/HUD · B07/U11 · B07/U10available towerTypes cards,cost/level/fireRate,drag,insufficient opacity,overflow; no paid context
OverlayScreenB05/U01 · B05/U02START start; other nonplaying end; localhost debug button unstyled/low priority
WaveRewardOverlayB06/U03 · B06/U04 · B06/U053 reward choices only; repair is alternate; unlock subsidy and blueprint upgrade from actual choice; future builds only
GameScreen paused modalB07/U06paused&&PLAYING&&!reward.active; continue/Esc
StatusBannerB07/U07only waveMsg.title/tone/accent; not invented extra subtitle
GameHud controls hintB07/U08 · B07/U09dismissible; mobile30s countdown; joystick touch origin is active-only
canvas dragPlacementB07/U10canPlace/range/worldX/worldY; invalid/insufficient actual messages
TowerContextMenudebug-only upgrade/downgrade; no normal-player paid upgrade
audio propsno currently rendered player controls; proposal-only deferred
DebugSpawnPanel/BossEditorPanel/PlaytestExportkeep usable; no product art packaging this revision
